## Tips
- The Mushroom Eater perk makes this pet the best for [[Mushroom]] collection and [[Coins]] earned from [[Farming]], even at 0 [[Strength]], assuming it is at least [[Rarity|RARE]] and [Lv41].
- Due to the Farming Strength perk, this pet has the potential to be the best for [[Farming Fortune]] for all crops, given sufficient [[Strength]]. However, in practice it is currently outclassed by the [[Rose Dragon Pet]] and [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Elephant Pet]] as well as the [[Hedgehog Pet]] for killing Pests. BonusFarmingFortune=⌊Strength20×0.7⌋
  - The formula for bonus [[Farming Fortune]] is as follows (brackets indicate rounding down):
  - A [Lv100] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Mooshroom Cow Pet provides the same [[Farming Fortune]] as a [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Elephant Pet]] at 1,428.58 [[Strength]], and 1 more at 1,457.15 [[Strength]].
  - A [Lv100] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Mooshroom Cow Pet provides the same [[Farming Fortune]] as a [Lv100] [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Elephant Pet]] at 2,857.15 [[Strength]], and 1 more at 2,885.72 [[Strength]].
- A [Lv100] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Mooshroom Cow Pet provides the same [[Farming Fortune]] against [[Pests]] as a [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Hedgehog Pet]] at 4,214.3 [[Strength]].
  - This is not feasible, as sacrificing other sources of [[Farming Fortune]] results in a net negative, and reaching that amount of [[Strength]] outside of dungeons is generally impossible.
- The highest obtainable amount of [[Strength]] as of 0.25, in a sensible Farming setup, under realistic conditions is as follows: 1,780.70 [[Strength]], totalling to 62 [[Farming Fortune]], and if [[Candidate Jerry]] is the active [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], the stats change to 1,955.75 (68 ). ## Trivia
- The [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]] dropped from the Mushroom Eater perk add to the Mushroom collection and the [[The Garden|Garden]] Milestone for Mushroom.
- The Mooshroom Cow used to be the best farming pet in the game immediately after its release with the Crimson Isle Update. The reason for this was that its perk Mushroom Eater would drop one of each mushroom per crop broken and they were affected by [[Farming Fortune]]. On top of that, the Farming Strength perk used to be +1 [[Farming Fortune]] every 10 [[Strength]] instead of the current +0.7 [[Farming Fortune]] every 20 [[Strength]] at level 100.
  - All of that combined, plus the fact that mushrooms have a very good [[NPC]] sell price, made the Mooshroom Cow pet the best farming pet in the game. Players with high strength optimization and high farming levels were able to get up to 12,000,000 [[Coins]] per hour easily.
## Bugs
- The Mushroom Eater perk grants double Mushroom drops when farming [[Cactus]] or [[Sugar Cane]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Mooshroom Cow Pet.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Removed the COMMON ([[Rarity]]) Efficient Mushrooms perk, which buffed Mycelium Minions ([[Mycelium Minion]]) and Mushroom Minions ([[Mushroom Minion]]) by up to 20%, or 30% for RARE ([[Rarity]]) and higher.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Lowered the Mushroom Eater perk requirement from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to COMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
|Jul 15, 2026|Lowered the Farming Strength perk requirement from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to RARE ([[Rarity]]).|
|Jul 15, 2026|Lowered the base [[Farming Fortune]] from 10 to 0, and therefore the range from 10-110 to 0-100.|

|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|8x [[Tarantula Silk]]|1 day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|16x [[Tarantula Silk]]|2 days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|32x [[Tarantula Silk]]|3 days|

## Tips
- A Mosquito Pet is recommended to swap to when the player's [[Pests|Pest]] cooldown is set to READY, due to its high [[Bonus Pest Chance]] and its Smooth Jazz perk, which increases the effects of [[Pest Vinyls]]. Once it spawns, the player should swap to a [[Hedgehog Pet]], or a pet with higher [[Farming Fortune]] if the player does not own one.
  - Despite being significantly more expensive, the [[Rarity|COMMON]] Mosquito Pet outclasses a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Slug Pet]] due to its +50% [[Bonus Pest Chance]] (compared to the [[Slug Pet]]'s +40% from Pest Friends) and its Smooth Jazz perk.
## Trivia
- The ability to craft the Mosquito Pet was explicitly disabled on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profiles until Patch 0.24.2, despite the ingredients being obtainable.

# Ocelot Pet
Aliases: Mystery Ocelot Pet
Categories: Pets, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Jungle Log Collection, Foraging pets
[Ocelot Pet]
Type: [[Foraging]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Jungle Log III (3) ([[Jungle Log]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Jungle Log]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: OCELOT
Tooltip: Foraging Pet; Ferocity: +0.1➡10; Speed: +0.5➡50; Foraging Wisdom Boost Grants +0.2➡20☯ Foraging Wisdom.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Ocelot is a craftable [[Foraging]] [[Pets|Pet]] unlocked at [[Jungle Log|Jungle Log III (3)]]. The crafting recipe requires 256 (4 stacks) [[Jungle Log]] and an [[Enchanted Egg]]. It is widely used by players because it grants additional [[Foraging Wisdom]] especially at [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarities. It is recommended not to go for the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant of this pet as the [[Rarity|EPIC]] variant already has the ability and takes less Pet XP to level up, unless you want the Tree Essence Ability.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Jungle Log III (3) ([[Jungle Log]])|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] 256x [[Jungle Log]]|Recipe: [[Jungle Log]] x32 ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [Lvl 1➡100] Ocelot|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|

|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|30 Minutes|

## Trivia
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Monkey Pet]] is better for [[Foraging]] exp, but the ocelot pet is a great budget alternative.
- The [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarities give the exact same amount of foraging XP as the third ability refers to vanilla enchanting XP, however the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] one is more difficult to level. The [[Rarity|RARE]] one also gives a buff in Foraging XP, but it's weaker.
- Its former ability, Evasion, has been moved to the [[Giraffe Pet]] and buffed to 0.25% per level instead of 0.2%.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|16x [[Floral Gelatin]]|1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|32x [[Floral Gelatin]]|2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|48x [[Floral Gelatin]]|3 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|2.5M [[Coins]]|64x [[Floral Gelatin]]|5 Days|
## Bugs
- Despite stating that it converts [[Speed]] into [[Farming Fortune]], this works differently from the [[Snail Pet]].
  - While the [[Snail Pet]] will take away the player's [[Speed]], the RARE or higher Orchid Mantis will not, despite having the same wording in its description.
  - Additionally, the Orchid Mantis will only count speed up to the player's speed cap, whereas the [[Snail Pet]] counts numbers past it.

|Jul 20, 2026|Swapped Swift Sickles and Intelligent Specimen.|

# Parrot Pet
Aliases: Parrot
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Dark Auction, Alchemy, Alchemy pets
[Parrot Pet]
Type: [[Alchemy]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: EPIC ([[Rarity]]), LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: [[Dark Auction]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: PARROT
Tooltip: Alchemy Pet; Crit Damage: +0.1➡10%; Intelligence: +1➡100; Flamboyant Adds 2➡15 levels to intimidation accessories.; Repeat Boosts potions duration by 5.35➡40%; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Parrot Pet is an [[Alchemy]] [[Pets|Pet]] that can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]].
## Obtaining
The Parrot Pet has a chance to appear in round 1, 3 or 4 of the [[Dark Auction]]. The [[Rarity|EPIC]] has an overall 22.706% chance to appear, and the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] has a 6.05% chance to appear.
Once the Parrot Pet appears, you must win the auction by paying more than all other players to obtain it.
## Stats
### Flamboyant Perk Scaling
The Flamboyant perk does not scale linearly, but is calculated using this formula:
  ⌈Pet Level100×(Maxed Value−1)+1⌉
  Maxed Value is the highest value of this variable. It differs between [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarities.
### Full Table
[[Rarity|EPIC]] Parrot Pet
|Starting Stats (Level 1) ⓘ (Stats of a pet at starting level)|Max Stats (Level 100) ⓘ (Stats of a maxed-out pet)VED|
|-|-|
|Stats: [[Crit Damage]]: +0.1% [[Intelligence]]: +1 Abilities: Flamboyant – Adds 2 level(s) to intimidation accessories ([[Accessories]]). Repeat – Boosts potions duration by 5.35%.|Stats: [[Crit Damage]]: +10% [[Intelligence]]: +100 Abilities: Flamboyant – Adds 15 level(s) to intimidation accessories ([[Accessories]]). Repeat – Boosts potions duration by 40%.|
[Leveling Bonusesⓘ (The bonuses gained with every pet level)]

[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Parrot Pet

|-|-|

Bird Discourse – Gives +5.25 [[Strength]] to players within 20 Blocks (doesn't stack). Parrot Feather Infusion – When summoned or in your pets menu, boost the duration of consumed God Potions ([[God Potion]]) by +0.2%.|Stats: [[Crit Damage]]: +10% [[Intelligence]]: +100 Abilities: Flamboyant – Adds 15 level(s) to intimidation accessories ([[Accessories]]). Repeat – Boosts potions duration by 40%. Bird Discourse – Gives +30 [[Strength]] to players within 20 Blocks (doesn't stack). Parrot Feather Infusion – When summoned or in your pets menu, boost the duration of consumed God Potions ([[God Potion]]) by +20%.|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|15M [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Feather]]|10 Days|

## Trivia
- The [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Spirit Pet]], [[Bingo Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Owl Pet]], and the Parrot Pet are the only pets that grant perks to the player even when they are not equipped.
- Parrots owned by players with any [[Ranks|Rank]] will occasionally repeat what they say in the chat. The chance of a parrot repeating the player's message is 12.5%.
  - This used to only apply to 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] and [[Ranks|Staff Members]].
- If multiple legendary Parrots are held in the same pet menu, only the one with the higher level will give the [[God Potion]] boost.
- Prior to the [[God Potion]]'s addition, this pet used to be used for god splashes for its duration extension effect.

# Pet Skins
Aliases: Golden Macaw Parrot Skin, Gym Rat Rat Skin, Henzo Doggo Skin, Junk Rat Rat Skin, Loyalty Kuudra Skin (Barbarian), Loyalty Kuudra Skin (Mage), Pet Skin, Shadow Wolf Skin, Snow Tiger Skin
Categories: Pets, Cosmetics
[Pet Skins]
Neon Red Ender Dragon Skin, a Pet Skin exclusive to Ender Dragon Pets.
Pet Skins are cosmetic items that can be applied to [[Pets]] to change both their texture and particle trail. Pet Skins can be applied by right-clicking on the player's active [[Pets|Pet]]. Once a Pet Skin is applied, it can no longer be taken off, meaning if the player applies a new skin, the previous one will be lost forever.

## Trivia
- An [[Rarity|EPIC]] pet skin for the [[Wolf Pet]] was found in 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] LadyBleu's Enderchest. It is called the Shadow Wolf Skin, and has the texture of the Twilight Tiger Pet Skin.
  - Interestingly, it was created on September 22, 2020, a month before the Twilight Tiger Pet Skin was added.
- The same texture of Beagle [[Hound Pet]] skin are used as [[Bingo]]'s head.
- The Enchanted and Seafoam skins for [[Armadillo Pet]] are likely based on pink fairy armadillos, the smallest species of armadillos with the resemblance in appearance of nigiri sushi.
- The Henzo Doggo Skin is a tribute to the passing of 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] hypixel's real life dog named Henzo.
- The RGBee skin for the [[Bee Pet]] is the only skin to have its texture changed.
- The Moocelium Mooshroom Cow Skin may be a reference to Purple Cow, a short nonsense poem by Gelett Burges.
- The Blue Moon, Nebula, and Sun Enderman skins are likely references of Mega Man Battle Network 4, a Gamboy Advance game with Blue Moon and Red Sun versions along with Nebula as the name of the group of main antagonists.
- The Loafed Tiger skin was originally for the [[Lion Pet]], but it was changed to the [[Tiger Pet]] before the sale started.

# Phoenix Pet
Aliases: Pheonix, Phoenix
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Combat pets, Mob Drops
[Phoenix Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])–VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Drop Chance: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]): 1 in 6,000,000; VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]): 1 in 30,000,000
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: Drop from any mob on any public island
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: PHOENIX
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Strength: +10.5➡60; Intelligence: +51➡150; Rekindle Before death, become immune and gain 10.1➡20  Strength for 2➡4 seconds.; 1 minute cooldown; Fourth Flare On 4th melee strike, ignite mobs, dealing 1.12➡13x your  Crit Damage each second for 2➡4 seconds.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; SPECIAL
The Phoenix Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] obtained by killing any mob on a public island and dungeons. It is an extremely rare drop. It does not grant [[Pets|Pet Score]], and therefore also no [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
## Obtaining
The Phoenix Pet is obtained as a rare drop from killing any mob.
|Rarity|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|0.000017% (1 in 6,000,000 kills)|
|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|0.000003% (1 in 30,000,000 kills)|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) → VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|100M [[Coins]]|1,024x (16 stacks (1,024 items total)) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]]|20 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ice Phoenix Skin]]|[[Ice Phoenix Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 25, 2021 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Flamingo Phoenix Skin]]|[[Flamingo Phoenix Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Spring 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Seagull Phoenix Skin]]|[[Seagull Phoenix Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - July 26, 2025 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- The [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] version of this pet is generally not worth it, as the additional perks it gives are also provided by a [[Booster Cookie]] (or a [[Piggy Bank]] and [[Magical Mushroom Soup]]).
## Trivia
- The first Phoenix Pet was dropped 5 minutes after Pets Update came out.
- There was a glitch in which the player could fly on public islands using the Phoenix Pet. It was fixed an hour after the update was released.
- For a time, it was said that this pet had a 0.1% chance of dropping from Special [[Zealot|Zealots]]; however, this has been debunked by admins.
- This was the first [[Pets|Pet]] added with 4 listed abilities.
- If a player obtains a Phoenix Pet, it is announced to the server with the following message: Wow! [player] found a Phoenix pet!
- [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] can also drop a Phoenix Pet.
- The Fourth Flare ability deals damage based on the player's [[Crit Damage]] stat, not the damage dealt when getting a critical hit.
- The first ability is currently bugged and doesn't protect you from fatal damage dealt by most dungeon mobs.

# Rock Pet
Aliases: Pet Rock, Pet rock
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Mining pets, 0.7.8, Mining
[Rock Pet]
Type: Mining Mount ([[Pets]]) Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head on Floor
Obtained: Mining ores ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: ROCK
Tooltip: Mining Mount; Defense: +2➡200; True Defense: +0.1➡10; Rideable Right-click your summoned pet to ride it!; Sailing Stone Sneak to move your rock to your location (15s cooldown).; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Rock Pet is a [[Pets|Mining Mount]] that drops directly from [[Mining]]. Unlike most pets, the Rock Pet is obtained via Mining milestones, similar to the [[Dolphin Pet]]. Upon reaching a milestone, a new Rock Pet is acquired, any previous Rock Pets are not upgraded.
## Obtaining
The Rock Pet is obtained by reaching [[Mining]] milestones. The milestones are as follows:
Note: To obtain the pet, players must mine a certain amount of [[Ores]], [[Dwarven Metals]] or [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
|Rarity|Ores Mined|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|2,500|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|7,500|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|20,000|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100,000|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|250,000|
If lost, the player can repurchase the highest rarity they have unlocked from [[Rusty]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]||1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|10M [[Coins]]||7 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50M [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|14 Days|

## Tips
- It is not recommended to upgrade this pet at [[Kat]], especially to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity, since it is very expensive and unnecessary.
- This pet is great for early game [[Enderman Slayer]], as it gives a massive amount of survivability while riding on it.
## Trivia
- Players used to have to use the [[SkyBlock API]] to see their milestone progress. However, this has been changed in SkyBlock Update 0.11.4, allowing the player to check the milestone progress in-game through the specific pet's appropriate skill menu once they have reached the first milestone.
- While riding the Rock Pet, the player takes no [[Knockback]]. This can be useful for [[Slayer]] quests and the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event.
- When sneaking at the edge of a block, the Rock Pet will fall down. Jumping after it and right-clicking it mid-air makes the player sit in the air not moving as the Rock Pet glitches away (it just falls down slowly now when you're on it instead of floating in the air).
- The Rock Pet is the first pet to have more than one skin in a [[Fire Sale]].
- There has been a bug involving using the Rock Pet and right clicking with an item with an item ability. This would cause the player to be bugged and be able to walk around, break blocks, and place blocks, all while still being on the rock pet on other player's screens. However, this appears to just be a visual bug, as other players still see the player on the rock pet. This bug was shown in a Dragonhanx YouTube video.
- Using [[Kat]] to upgrade a Rock Pet means that, when you reach a milestone, you end up with another Rock Pet, meaning you could potentially end up with 5 [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Rock Pets. The amount of effort, time and money required to obtain 5 [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Rock Pets is prohibitive, and is not recommended, as it doesn't give you additional [[Pets|Pet Score]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Rock Pet.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|[[Blocks]] now count towards Rock Pet milestones.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed dismounting a Rock Pet at Y=1 teleporting the player to the top of the world.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|[[Blocks]], [[Dwarven Metals]] and Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) no longer count towards Rock Pet milestones.|
|Nov 19, 2024|0.20.8|[[Dwarven Metals]] and Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) count towards Rock Pet milestones once again.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Added Rock Pet to [[Rusty]].|

Tooltip: Mining Pet; Mining Speed: +1➡100; Mining Fortune: +1.25➡125; Burrowing Grants a +0.3➡30% chance to find Treasure Chests while mining.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; RARE
The Scatha Pet is a [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]] that grants [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]] to the player.
## Obtaining
It is rarely dropped from [[Scatha|Scathas]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]], found when mining [[Hard Stone]].
|Pet Rarity|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|0.144% (0.24% in Bestiary) (1 in 694.4)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|0.072% (0.12% in Bestiary) (1 in 1388.9)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|0.024% (0.04% in Bestiary) (1 in 4166.7)|
|Any|0.24% (1 in 416.7)|
- The drop chances are not reduced to 1/5th from Loot Sharing.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.

|-|-|-|-|

## Tips
- Equipping a [[Hephaestus Relic]] grants 12.5 [[Mining Fortune]] more than a [[Quick Claw]].
## Trivia
- The Scatha is a reference to the wingless dragon Scatha from The Lord of the Rings.
- The Bejeweled Eyes ability is additive with the Powder Buff perk from the [[Heart of the Mountain]].
- Prior to the October 5th 2026 update, only 1 [[Scatha Pet]] Scatha Pet could drop from a single [[Scatha]] among all its contributors.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Scatha Pet.|
|Apr 1, 2024|Fixed the Wormhole perk working on [[Titanium]] and [[Tungsten]].|
|Aug 20, 2024|Removed base [[Defense]].|
|Aug 20, 2024|Removed Grounded perk and moved [[Mining Fortune]] to base stats.|

|Aug 20, 2024|Replaced Wormhole perk with Drill Infusion.|
|Aug 20, 2024|Renamed Gemstone Power to Bejeweled Eyes.|
|Oct 9, 2024|Fixed being able to get guaranteed Loot Share ([[Loot Sharing]]) on a [[Scatha Pet]] Scatha Pet.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Multiplied chance for the pet to drop from Scatha by 2.5x.|

|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Scatha Pet.|

|Feb 2, 2022|Multiplied chance for the pet to drop from Scatha by 4x.|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Raw Mutton]] 512x White Wool ([[Wool]])|5 Minutes|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] 8x [[Enchanted Wool]]|10 Minutes|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] 16x [[Enchanted Wool]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] 32x [[Enchanted Wool]]|30 Minutes|

## Trivia
- There is a bug where the Overheal ability will always be maxed out regardless of pet level.
- This is the only craftable [[Alchemy]] pet.
- The Sheep Pet has the most pet skins in the game, at a total of 13.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|500 [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Cobblestone]]|10 Seconds|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|2.5k [[Coins]]|256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Cobblestone]]|20 Seconds|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Cobblestone]]|30 Seconds|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|45 Seconds|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Fossilized Silverfish Skin]]|[[Fossilized Silverfish Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - January 22, 2021 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Fortified Silverfish Skin]]|[[Fortified Silverfish Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 22, 2020 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Silbrrrfish Silverfish Skin]]|[[Silbrrrfish Silverfish Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - January 18, 2025 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Trivia
- On islands with custom mining mechanics, such as the [[Dwarven Mines]], the [[Haste Potion|Haste]] effect does not apply, and is converted into extra [[Mining Speed]] instead. This does not stack with the conversion of [[Haste Potion|Haste]] from other sources.
- At [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity, the [[Haste Potion|Haste]] effect level scales as follows:
|Pet Level|Haste ([[Haste Potion]]) Level|
|-|-|
|[Lv1] → [Lv50]|1 ([[Haste Potion]])|
|[Lv51] → [Lv99]|2 ([[Haste Potion]])|
|[Lv100]|3 ([[Haste Potion]])|

# Skeleton Pet
Aliases: Skeleton Pet Egg
Categories: Pets, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Bone Collection, Combat pets, Dungeons
[Skeleton Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Bone III (3) ([[Bone]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Bone]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: SKELETON
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Crit Chance: +0.15➡15%; Crit Damage: +0.3➡30%; Bone Arrows Increase arrow damage by 0.1➡10%; which is doubled while in dungeons.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Skeleton Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] unlocked at [[Bone|Bone III (3)]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Bone]]|5 Minutes|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Bone]]|10 Minutes|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Bone]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Bone]]|30 Minutes|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pearl Skeleton Skin]]|[[Pearl Skeleton Skin]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2024 - 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin adapts to the day-night cycle!|
|[[Pearl Skeleton Skin]]|[[Pearl Skeleton Skin]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2024 - 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Santa Skeleton Skin]]|[[Santa Skeleton Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Holidays 2025 - 100 Event Silver||
|[[Withering Skeleton Skin]]|[[Withering Skeleton Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Halloween 2026 - 100 Event Silver||
## Trivia
- The Bone Arrows perk, unlike most percentage increases, is additive.
- The Bone Arrows perk is bugged to not triple its effectiveness within [[Dungeons]].
- When wearing a full set of [[Wither Armor]] or its variants, the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] pet's arrow will always target the wither spawned from the Witherborn full set ability.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Skeleton Pet.|
|May 21, 2020|Changed Skeletal Defense's arrow from dealing 5x its user's final damage to 30x its user's [[Crit Damage]].|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Reduced Skeletal Defense cooldown from 15s to 5s.|

The Sloth Pet is a [[Foraging]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Sloth Pet can be purchased from [[Miria]] for 20 [[Miria's Coupon|Miria's Coupons]]. Higher rarities are obtained through [[Kat]] by using Sloth Claws purchased from [[Miria]].

Pet ID: SLUG
Tooltip: Farming Pet; Defense: +0.2➡20; Intelligence: +0.25➡25; Slow and Steady When fishing in the Crimson Isle, Slugfish take 0.5➡50%; less time to catch.; Pest Friends Grants +0.4➡40 Bonus Pest Chance.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Slug Pet is a [[Farming]] [[Pets|Pet]]. It is primarily useful for increasing [[Bonus Pest Chance]]. It can also be used to catch [[Trophy Fish|Slugfish]] quicker.
## Obtaining
The Slug Pet can be obtained by killing [[Slug|Slugs]].

|[Lv1] [[Slug]]|1%; (1 in 100)|0.2%; (1 in 500)|

|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10M [[Coins]]|1x [[Compost Bundle]]|7 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Violet Sea Slug Skin]]|[[Violet Sea Slug Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 2, 2023 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Leaf Sheep Sea Slug Skin]]|[[Leaf Sheep Sea Slug Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 2, 2023 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Azure Sea Slug Skin]]|[[Azure Sea Slug Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 2, 2023 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Banana Slug Skin]]|[[Banana Slug Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - February 22, 2025 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- A Slug Pet is recommended to swap to when the player's [[Pests|Pest]] cooldown is set to READY, due to its high [[Bonus Pest Chance]]. Once it spawns, the player should swap to a [[Hedgehog Pet]], or a pet with higher [[Farming Fortune]] if the player does not own one.
  - A [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Slug Pet is outclassed by a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mosquito Pet]]: however, it is also much cheaper.
- The Slug Pet's Repugnant Aroma perk is doubled by the [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]]'s effects, making it useful over a [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] for [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] and high collections.
  - However, it remains less useful for making money over a [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] due to the latter's Mushroom Eater perk.
## Trivia
- Slug Pet's Repugnant Aroma ability is affected by the [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]], meaning the ability can give up to 200 , making it the second strongest pet for [[Farming Fortune]].

|Nov 12, 2023|Increased EPIC ([[Rarity]]) drop rate from 0.25% to 0.5%.|
|Nov 12, 2023|Increased LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) drop rate from 0.05% to 0.1%.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Red Sand]]|1 Hour|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Red Sand]]|3 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 32x [[Burning Eye]]|6 Hours|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 64x [[Burning Eye]]|12 Hours|

## Tips
- Every [[Speed]] buff, even beyond the speed cap, counts towards the Slow and Steady ability.
- As of SkyBlock 0.20.6, since [[Blocks]] are now also affected by regular [[Mining Fortune]], players need to balance the two stats, and only sacrifice [[Mining Fortune]] if they gain more than twice as much [[Speed]] as the [[Mining Fortune]] sacrificed.
  - [[Rancher's Boots]] is generally a good choice, especially if the player has a high [[Farming]] level.
    - The customized speed cap of the [[Rancher's Boots]] doesn't affect the [[Mining Fortune]] given.
- Using the Snail Pet is generally not recommended anymore for certain blocks such as [[Mycelium]] and [[Red Sand]] due to the high amount of [[Mining Spread]] players can get making the 100 [[Speed]] cap affect rates negatively.
- Things that can be used to increase the player's [[Speed]] include:
  - Abilities that give [[Speed]] such as [[Rogue Sword]], [[Weird Tuba]] and [[Aspect of the End]].
    - The [[Aspect of the End]]'s buff has a very short duration, however the duration stacks if used multiple times in quick succession.
  - [[Accessories]] with [[Speed]] buffs.
  - Speed [[Enrichments|Enrichment]] (+1 [[Speed]] per [[Accessories|accessory]]).
  - [[Powers|Sweet]] Power and [[Maxwell|Speed Tuning]].
  - [[Beacon|Beacon V]] with a [[Scorched Power Crystal]] and the [[Speed]] stat selected..
  - [[Attributes|Speed attribute]] on [[Equipment]].
## Trivia
- It used to be possible to get ∞ [[Speed]] and therefore ∞ [[Block Fortune]].
- Snail Pet is the only[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] pet that has a pet item it itself cannot equip, the pet item being [[Unalloyed Speed]].

## Obtaining
The Squid Pet can rarely be fished as a [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]] or an [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCH]] anywhere in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] (except in the [[Tomb Floodway]]).

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20k [[Coins]]||1 Hour|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]||6 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|3M [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Ink Sac]]|5 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Glow Squid Skin]]|[[Glow Squid Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - September 14, 2021 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[Glow Squid Skin]]|[[Glow Squid Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - September 14, 2021 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Chromari Squid Skin]]|[[Chromari Squid Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - June 8, 2024 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[Chromari Squid Skin]]|[[Chromari Squid Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - June 8, 2024 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Goblin Squid Skin]]|[[Goblin Squid Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Spring 2025 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Tips
- When trying to obtain this pet, it is best to do so in the [[Backwater Bayou]], as the highest possible [[Treasure Chance]] is obtainable there.
- A recommendation would be to use a [[Rarity|RARE]] or better [[Hermit Crab Pet]] for its Seafloor Scalper perk which increases chances of getting great or outstanding catches for treasure.
- A [[Junk Sinker]] should not be used when going for this pet, as it will prevent it from being obtained.
- The Ink Speciality ability isn't very useful as it only buffs the [[Ink Wand]]'s melee damage, which it has very little of. The [[Ink Wand]] is much more useful for [[Ability Damage]].
## Trivia
- This pet used to have the More Squids ability in which, instead of increasing the chance of double drops, it increased the chance to find squids; it also had the Fishing Exp Boost ability that granted a 30% increase to Fishing Exp gained rather than [[Fishing Wisdom]].

## Tips
- Despite being the best pet for [[Ferocity]], this pet has incredibly limited usage. [[Ferocity]] doesn't properly work with bows, because the extra hit counts as a melee hit, and as such isn't booster by [[Power]] and other effects for bows, and is heavily nerfed using it with melee weapons as [[Berserk]] in [[Dungeons]]. Players should use a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Lion Pet]] for melee or a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Skeleton Pet]] for Archer.
## Trivia
- The Tiger Pet's Merciless Swipe ability does not increase [[Ferocity]] by a set number; instead, it multiplies the player's [[Ferocity]]. For example, a Level 100 Tiger Pet will grant 32.5 [[Ferocity]], not 55.
- The Tiger Pet's Apex Predator plays a sound effect when hitting isolated mobs.
- The Tiger Pet used to be used in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor IV]], as a bug caused [[Ferocity]] granted by the Tiger Pet to hit [[Thorn]] twice.

|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Increased base [[Ferocity]] by 0.15 per level (from 0.1 to 0.25).|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Decreased Merciless Swipe's [[Ferocity]] increase to +0.15/+0.33/+0.33/+0.5/+0.5 per level (from 0.2/0.5/0.5/1/1).|
|Feb 2, 2022|Decreased Merciless Swipe's [[Ferocity]] increase to +0.1/+0.2/+0.2/+0.3/+0.3 per level (from +0.15/+0.33/+0.33/+0.5/+0.5).|

# Turtle Pet
Aliases: Turtle
Categories: Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Dark Auction, Combat pets
[Turtle Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: EPIC ([[Rarity]]) and LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: [[Dark Auction]]
[Item Properties]

Tooltip: Combat Pet; Health: +50.25➡75; Defense: +100.5➡150; True Defense: +0.15➡15; Turtle Tactics Gain +3.27➡30%  Defense and an additional +10%  Defense when standing still.; Genius Amniote Grants +1.01➡2.5%  Defense to 4; players within 50 blocks of you.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; EPIC
The Turtle Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] that can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]]. Like all pets from the Dark Auction, the Turtle Pet only comes in two rarities: [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
## Obtaining
The Turtle Pet has a chance to appear in round 1, 3 or 4 of the [[Dark Auction]]. The [[Rarity|EPIC]] has an overall 22.706% chance to appear, and the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] has a 6.05% chance to appear.

Type: Gabagool ([[Crude Gabagool]]) Pet ([[Pets]])

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: DROPLET_WISP; FROST_WISP; GLACIAL_WISP; SUBZERO_WISP
Tooltip: Gabagool Pet, feed to gain XP; Health: +1➡100; Damage: +0.1➡10; Drophammer Lets you break fire pillars 2x faster, healing you for 15% of your max  over 3s.; Bulwark Kill Blazes to gain defense against them and demons.; Bonus: +0 & +0 Next Upgrade: +30 & +3 (0\ 100); Blaze Slayer Gain +0.3➡30☯ Combat Wisdom towards Blazes.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; UNCOMMON
The Wisp Pet is a craftable [[Crude Gabagool|Gabagool]] [[Pets|Pet]]: rather than gaining XP traditionally, it must be fed Gabagool to level up, and resets its level upon gaining a rarity. It is designed for [[Blaze Slayer]].
## Obtaining
The Wisp Pet is obtained as a craftable pet at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL I (1)]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer I (1) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Omega Enchanted Egg]] 8x [[Derelict Ashe]]|Recipe: [[Derelict Ashe]] ×8 + [[Omega Enchanted Egg]] → [[Wisp Pet]]|
## Upgrading
The Wisp Pet cannot be upgraded at [[Kat]], and must be upgraded with [[Wisp Upgrade Stone|Wisp Upgrade Stones]]. Each Wisp Upgrade Stone can be crafted with drops from the [[Inferno Demonlord]].
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Kelvin Inverter]] 1x [[Inferno Vertex]] 3x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] ×3 + [[Inferno Vertex]] + [[Kelvin Inverter]] → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|6x [[Kelvin Inverter]] 8x [[Inferno Vertex]] 12x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 48x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Inferno Vertex]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x6 ×2 + [[Molten Powder]] x24 ×2 + [[Kelvin Inverter]] x6 → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Subzero Inverter]] 1x [[Inferno Apex]] 2x [[Bluetooth Ring]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×2 + [[Inferno Apex]] + [[Bluetooth Ring]] ×2 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Subzero Inverter]] + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x64 → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
Upon gaining a rarity, the Wisp Pet gains a new prefix and resets its level.

## Trivia
- The Wisp Pet was the first pet added to not have an attributed [[Skills|Skill]].
- It is also the first pet that doesn't level up through gaining skill experience.
  - It is also the first pet that requires a consumable item to level up.
  - The pet experience gained from feeding is affected by [[Derpy|Mayor Derpy]]'s MOAR SKILLZ!!! perk, which reduces the required amount of [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]] items by around 33.3% during his mayorship.
  - Since feeding the [[Pets|pet]] will consume the entire stack held in hand, it is possible to overfeed the pet right before it reaches level 100.
- The Wisp Pet is the only pet that changes name depending on its [[Rarity]].
- The Wisp Pet is the only pet that has a level requirement to be upgraded.
- If one buys a Wisp Pet from the [[Auction House]] or through trading, without the proper Blaze Slayer level, its perks wouldn't activate.
- The Wisp Pet is the only pet that starts as at [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]].
- The [[Attributes|Battle Experience]] attribute will affect XP gained to a Wisp Pet.

|Sep 21, 2026|Blaze Slayer now grants +50 [[Combat Wisdom]] instead of 1.5x Combat XP.|

|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Wisp Pet.|

|Aug 5, 2022|Renamed Coldfusion to Ephemeral Stability.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Added +10/15/20/25 base damage.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Upgraded Icehammer to provide healing over time after breaking a pillar.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Each upgrade recipe has been updated to be generally much cheaper.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|The Wisp Pet is no longer required to break [[Inferno Demonlord]] Fire Pillars.|

Aliases: Mystery Wither Skeleton Pet, Wither Pet, Wither pet

The Wither Skeleton is a craftable [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]] unlocked at [[Coal|Coal IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Coal IV (4) ([[Coal]])|1x [[Super Enchanted Egg]] 8x [[Enchanted Coal]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Coal]] ×8 + [[Super Enchanted Egg]] → [Lvl 1➡100] Wither Skeleton|
## Stats
### Notes
- The Death's Touch ability activates upon hitting any enemy, except against the [[Revenant Horror|Atoned Horror]], the [[Sven Packmaster]] and [[Sven Packmaster|Sven Pups]]. It is not activated by abilities.
- The damage of the 1st hit is copied and multiplied by the listed percentage. Then, 1/3rd of that damage is dealt as [[Damage Calculation|Wither Damage]] 3 times: at the same time as the initial hit, 1 second after, and 2 seconds after. At [Lv100], this is 2/3rds of the initial damage per damage tick.
- The ability cannot activate again or stack during the 2 seconds unless the mob disappears (for example, by getting absorbed by a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]]).

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Coal]]|1 Hour|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Coal]]|3 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Coal]]|6 Hours|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Coal]]|12 Hours|

## Tips
- At [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity, the Death's Touch perk greatly improves the hunting experience as the [[Damage Calculation|Wither Damage]] works while holding [[Huntaxes]].
## Trivia
- Although the Wither Skeleton pet is a [[Mining]] pet, its abilities seem to be more useful for [[Combat]].
- While Pets V2 was being rolled out, a bug was found that allowed players to kill other players using the Death's Touch Ability outside of the Colosseum.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Wither Skeleton Pet.|
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Coal Crazy ability replaced with Death's Touch.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Changed Wither Blood's damage bonus against wither mobs from 50% to 25%.|
